- **cuda-occupancy-register-pressure-gb10**: Occupancy is the ratio of active warps to the maximum the SM can hold. Higher occupancy gives the warp scheduler more threads to switch to when one warp stalls, hiding latency. But chasing 100% occupancy can backfire: it forces the compiler to use fewer registers per thread, which can spill to local memory and slow things down. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/cuda-occupancy-register-pressure-gb10) · ★1 · 2 copies)

Not for cuDF-only dataframe work, PyTorch or XGBoost. ([dannywise093-crypto/NEXORA-AI](https://github.com/dannywise093-crypto/NEXORA-AI/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/cuml) · 4 copies)

- **cumulant-order-block-sparse-attention**: COBS - block sparse attention selector that stores a compressed SECOND-order (cumulant) statistic per block instead of only first-order (attention mass), closing the gap to dense attention at ~15x less KV-cache read traffic.
